([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-mass-spectra-distortion-simulation))
- **tandem-mass-spectra-dot-product-computation**: Use when pairwise distances between proteomics samples must be quantified directly from peak-listed tandem mass spectra (MGF) without peptide identification or spectral libraries, e.g. to build a distance matrix for molecular phylogenetics or data quality control. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-mass-spectra-dot-product-computation))
- **tandem-mass-spectra-embedding-evaluation**: Use when you have embedded a set of tandem mass spectra (e.g. NIST20, MoNA) into a vector space and need to quantify whether that space reflects molecular structural similarity or supports analog retrieval. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-mass-spectra-embedding-evaluation))
- **tandem-mass-spectra-embedding-generation**: Use when you have tandem mass spectra (in .msp or compatible format) from instruments like Orbitrap, and you need to compute library matching scores, cluster spectra by chemical similarity, or embed spectra into a learned vector space for downstream similarity or clustering tasks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-mass-spectra-embedding-generation) · 2 copies)
- **tandem-mass-spectra-fragmentation-parsing**: Use when when you have raw tandem mass spectra in mz/intensity format with precursor m/z values, and need to extract all fragmentation features (observed peaks and neutral losses) as a foundation for building interpretable machine learning models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-mass-spectra-fragmentation-parsing) · 2 copies)
- **tandem-mass-spectra-interpretation**: Use when you have an unknown MS/MS spectrum (tandem mass spectrum) with a measured precursor m/z and fragment peaks, and you need to assign the most likely molecular formula and ionization adduct (e.g., [M+H]+, [M+Na]+, [M+K]+). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-mass-spectra-interpretation) · 3 copies)
- **tandem-mass-spectra-library-preparation**: Use when raw GNPS/MassIVE .mzML experiments must be converted into deduplicated, quality-filtered, paired or unpaired MS/MS spectral libraries (e.g. GeMS, MoNA, NIST20) for self-supervised pre-training or supervised fine-tuning of spectrum annotation models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-mass-spectra-library-preparation))
- **tandem-mass-spectra-peptide-matching**: Use when you have centroided LC-MS/MS spectra (in MGF, mzXML, mzML, or mzData format) and wish to identify peptidic natural products or ribosomally synthesized and post-translationally modified peptides (RiPPs) against a known structure database or custom RiPP structure database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-mass-spectra-peptide-matching) · 3 copies)
- **tandem-mass-spectra-prediction**: Use when you have a molecular structure dataset (e.g., NIST20 labels.tsv + mgf_files + spec_files.hdf5) and need to predict MS/MS spectra or compare spectrum simulators under matched covariates and hyperparameter sweeps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-mass-spectra-prediction))
- **tandem-mass-spectra-preprocessing**: Use when raw MS/MS spectra in MGF format must be converted to h5py and normalized/denoised before spectral similarity computation, spectral library search, or training/testing TransExION on GNPS/GNPS_MassBank/mergedGNPS data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-mass-spectra-preprocessing))
- **tandem-mass-spectra-research**: Guides research comparison of tandem mass spectra and spectral-library evidence. Use when preparing MS/MS data, matching spectra or interpreting candidate compound identities. ([dandgabr/Coacus](https://github.com/dandgabr/Coacus/tree/HEAD/knowledge/skills/domains/academic/tandem-mass-spectra-research) · ★10)
- **tandem-mass-spectra-set-overlap-analysis**: Use when two or more LC-MS/MS datasets exported as MGF tandem mass spectra need a set-level similarity and distance (not peptide identification) — e.g. species identification, mixture characterization, phylogenetics, or batch-effect QC on spectral content. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-mass-spectra-set-overlap-analysis))
- **tandem-mass-spectra-similarity-scoring**: Use when you have positive and/or negative ionization mode MS/MS peak spectra (mgf, msp, mzML, mzXML, json, usi) and need predicted chemical similarity scores, e.g., to build a unified cross-mode molecular network. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-mass-spectra-similarity-scoring))
- **tandem-mass-spectra-standardization**: Use when you have acquired raw tandem MS data from ProteomeXchange or vendor instruments in proprietary formats (e.g., Thermo .raw files) and need to perform comparative clustering benchmarks or quality assessments across multiple clustering tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-mass-spectra-standardization) · 2 copies)
- **tandem-mass-spectra-structural-similarity-assessment**: Use when you have pairs of cleaned tandem mass spectra with known chemical structure annotations (InChIKey, SMILES, or InChI), and you need to predict Tanimoto structural similarity scores directly from spectral data without pre-computing molecular fingerprints. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-mass-spectra-structural-similarity-assessment) · 2 copies)
- **tandem-mass-spectral-dereplication**: Use when experimental tandem mass spectra of natural products (e.g. NRPs/PK siderophores like desferrioxamine B) must be matched against a custom in-silico fragment database to identify the compound producing each spectrum. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-mass-spectral-dereplication))
- **tandem-mass-spectrometry-data-interpretation**: Use when you have high-resolution MS2 data (.ms2 format) from tandem mass spectrometry analysis of lipid A-containing samples and need to perform automated structure annotation and identification at systems scale. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrometry-data-interpretation) · 2 copies)
- **tandem-mass-spectrometry-data-processing**: Use when multiple ESI-MS2 records of a single compound exist at different collision-induced dissociation energies and you must merge them into one reference spectrum or score searches with a weighted cosine correlation optimized for ESI-MS2 data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrometry-data-processing))
- **tandem-mass-spectrometry-fragment-matching**: Use when pairwise alignment of tandem MS/MS fragmentation spectra (m/z arrays with precursor m/z) must be scored with statistical significance (z-test p-values) for structurally related metabolites, e.g. in molecular networking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrometry-fragment-matching))
- **tandem-mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-simulation**: Use when you have a new fragmentation acquisition strategy (e.g., a weighted exclusion variant, alternative TopN ranking, or dynamic isolation window rule) that you wish to evaluate without access to real mass spectrometry hardware. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrometry-fragmentation-simulation) · 3 copies)
- **tandem-mass-spectrometry-ion-fragmentation-pattern-analysis**: Use when after MS1 feature extraction and prescreening quality control have completed on mzML files, and you need to inspect the MS2 fragmentation patterns of candidate compounds to verify their identity or assess whether extracted features are genuine metabolites rather than noise or artifacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrometry-ion-fragmentation-pattern-analysis) · 2 copies)
- **tandem-mass-spectrometry-metadata-standardization**: Use when you have raw MS/MS spectra from public repositories (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrometry-metadata-standardization) · 2 copies)
- **tandem-mass-spectrometry-mirror-plot-construction**: Use when when you have raw LC-MS or LC-IMS-MS data in instrument format (Agilent .d, Thermo .raw, Bruker . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrometry-mirror-plot-construction) · 4 copies)
- **tandem-mass-spectrometry-peptide-identification**: Use when MS/MS spectra must be identified against a peptide sequence database, especially when mutations or unexpected post-translational modifications may be present or spectra are of low quality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrometry-peptide-identification))
- **tandem-mass-spectrometry-spectrum-processing**: Use when raw tandem MS (MS2/MSn) spectra—especially low-resolution spectra from a miniaturized mass spectrometer—must be converted into candidate molecular structures and scored by fingerprint Tanimoto similarity against known SMILES. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrometry-spectrum-processing))
- **tandem-mass-spectrometry-spectrum-representation**: Use when you need to convert raw tandem mass spectrometry (MS/MS) peak lists into a de novo-style spectrum graph that a sequence-database search or ACO-based parser can traverse, especially for peptides with mutations or unexpected PTMs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrometry-spectrum-representation))
- **tandem-mass-spectrum-clustering**: Use when you have a large collection of tandem mass spectra (mzML, mzXML, or MGF format) and want to group similar spectra into clusters to identify redundancy, discover novel peptides or metabolites, or prepare data for downstream annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrum-clustering) · 3 copies)
- **tandem-mass-spectrum-clustering**: Use when raw shotgun proteomics MS/MS data (e.g. PRIDE PXD000561-style datasets with millions of spectra in mzML, mzXML, or MGF files) must be reduced to cluster representatives for more efficient downstream identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrum-clustering))
- **tandem-mass-spectrum-counting**: Use when you need to describe the size of a set of LC-MS/MS acquisitions, e.g. reporting the default compareMS2 QC metric or validating that downloaded ProteomeXchange files are complete. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrum-counting))
- **tandem-mass-spectrum-database-search**: Use when LC-MS/MS spectra (MGF, mzXML, mzML, or mzData) need to be identified against a database of known natural product structures, especially when exact-formula dereplication by precursor mass alone is ambiguous. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrum-database-search))
- **tandem-mass-spectrum-decoding**: Use when when you have raw predictions from a trained fragment generation or intensity prediction neural network model and need to convert those predictions into a standard spectrum file format (m/z–intensity pairs) for comparison against experimental spectra or for structural elucidation workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrum-decoding) · 2 copies)
- **tandem-mass-spectrum-deconvolution-isotope-annotation**: Use when you have aligned features characterized across multiple dimensions (m/z, drift time, retention time) and need to: (1) resolve MS/MS spectra that may contain fragments from multiple co-eluting or co-mobilizing precursors; (2) identify and validate isotopic signatures (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrum-deconvolution-isotope-annotation) · 4 copies)
- **tandem-mass-spectrum-normalization**: Use when preparing tandem MS/MS datasets for cross-dataset similarity analysis or spectral matching, particularly when datasets originate from different instruments, acquisition dates, or sample preparation protocols that may introduce systematic variations in peak intensities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrum-normalization) · 2 copies)
- **tandem-mass-spectrum-parsing**: Use when when you have raw or instrument-native tandem mass spectrometry data (MS/MS) in formats such as mzML, mzXML, or proprietary binary formats, and you need to align, match, or compare spectra using methods like SIMILE that require structured access to precursor m/z, fragment m/z values, and. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrum-parsing) · 2 copies)
- **tandem-mass-spectrum-prediction-fragment-level**: Use when you have a molecular structure (SMILES, InChI, or chemical formula) and need to predict its collision-induced dissociation (CID) tandem mass spectrum with fragment-level resolution. Use this when chemical-formula-level predictions (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrum-prediction-fragment-level) · 2 copies)
- **tandem-mass-spectrum-preprocessing-and-normalization**: Use when you have acquired raw MS/MS spectra (in MGF or mzML format) from a mass spectrometry instrument or public repository (e.g., MassIVE, MetaboLights, GNPS) that will be used for de novo chemical formula ranking or adduct assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tandem-mass-spectrum-preprocessing-and-normalization) · 3 copies)
- **tandem-ms-accuracy-benchmarking**: Use when comparing MS/MS formula-prediction methods on a labeled test set (e.g. NPLIB1, CASMI2022) and need rigorous metrics such as top-1 accuracy, joint formula+adduct accuracy, and per-spectrum concordance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-accuracy-benchmarking))
- **tandem-ms-annotation-score-extraction**: Use when a feature-level annotation table contains top candidate structures and scores from multiple MS/MS annotation tools (GNPS FBMN, Sirius 6, ISDB-LOTUS) and per-ion agreement/match indicators are needed for prioritization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tandem-ms-annotation-score-extraction))
